                jQuery("div.HeadDiv div.mobileHeadButton").removeClass("active");
                jQuery(this).addClass("active");
                jQuery("div.HeadDiv div."+jQuery(this).attr("rel")).addClass("active");
                jQuery("#TopBannerTitulka1").css({"visibility":"hidden"});
            }
        });

            function UniModalDialogReset() {
                unimodal_dialog = jQuery( "#unimodal_dialog" ).dialog({
                    title: '',
                    dialogClass: '',
                    autoOpen: false,
                    height: ((window.innerHeight < 800) ? window.innerHeight - 50 : 800),

                        }
                    },
                    close: function() {
                        UniModalDialogReset();
                    }
                });

            }
